Example B.3

(Merging via subtyping)
.

The following typing involves
merging

where the behaviour of two or more channel vector types in the branches
are mixed into one, as

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}}\vdash_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{choice}}\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{finish}}}}\right)}}{\texttt{,}\,}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}\ {\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{finish}}}}\right)}}\right\}}}\mathbin{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}}

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}}

where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}{:}\bigl\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}{:}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}\rangle\rangle},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}{:}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}\rangle\rangle}}}}}}\bigr\rangle\Bigr\rangle}
,
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}]}}\rangle}
.
See that the continuations
({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{finish}}}})
and

({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{finish}}}})

have the channel vector types

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}]}}\rangle}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}]}}\rangle}

at role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}
respectively,
where each of them receives label
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}
from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}
.
By subtyping, they are amalgamated into a
common super type

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}]}}\rangle}

which can now receive both labels.
This is underpinned by the subtyping relation as

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}]}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}}]}}\rangle}

(similar for
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}
)
which is justified by
[
Otg-Sub
]
.

Example B.4

(Recursion)
.

The following typing derivation is valid under
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathbb{R}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}}\vdash_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathbb{R}}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{fix}}\,{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}\,\texttt{-{}>}\,{\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}}\right)}}\right)}}}\mathbin{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\left(\noindent\hbox{}{{\hbox{$\begin{array}[c]{@{}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}{.}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}{:}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}{:}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}}}\rangle\Bigr\rangle}}}}\rangle\Bigr\rangle}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}\\
\ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}{.}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}}}]}}\Bigr\rangle}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}}}{.}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}}}}}]}}\Bigr\rangle}}}\end{array}$}}}\hfill\hbox{}\right)}
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}}}}\vdash_{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathbb{R}}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}}\right)}}}\mathbin{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}{:}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{a}}}}}{:}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}}}\rangle\Bigr\rangle}}}}\rangle\Bigr\rangle}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Bigl\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip Example B.5

(Loops and the finished session)
.

The following example shows the usage of the
function
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\mathrm{tfix}}{\left({\cdot},{\cdot}\right)}}
in the rule
[
Otg
-
fix
]

comes from the corresponding case in the End Point Projection in MPST
[
56
]
.It declares the termination of the session for a role
in a loop in which the role in question
never participate in.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}}\vdash_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}{\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{fix}}\,{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}\,\texttt{-{}>}\,{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\texttt{)}\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}}}}\right)}\mathbin{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{.}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}{:}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}\rangle\rangle}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}{.}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{:}{{{?}[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}}{\times}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}]}}\rangle}}}}

where the channel vector type for
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
is the finished session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}

because
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\mathrm{tfix}}{\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\right)}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bullet}
.

Appendix C
MiO
: A minimal
ocaml-mpst
calculus

We introduces a minimal functional calculus,
MiO
and its typing systems.
The calculus distils the main features required for embedding session types in OCaml,
notably equi-recursive types, record and variant types, structural subtyping, and simply-typed I/O channels.
We prove the type soundness for
MiO
(Theorem
C.8
).

C.1
MiO
: Syntax and Dynamic Semantics

This section introduces the syntax and
operational semantics of
MiO
.

C.1.1
MiO
Program

We introduce the syntax of
MiO
program, which is written
by the programmer.

Definition C.1

(
MiO
program)
.

The
program
(or expression) of
MiO
is defined as:

\begin{array}[]{c|c}\begin{array}[]{rcll}&&\hskip-8.53581pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\quad{::=}\\
&&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}},\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}&\text{\footnotesize(initiation)}\\
&&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}\boldsymbol{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\boldsymbol{\#}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}&\text{\footnotesize(send)}\\
&&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}\boldsymbol{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}&\text{\footnotesize(receive)}\\
&&\hskip-8.53581pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v}\quad{::=}\quad x,y,z,...{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}&\text{\footnotesize(values)}\\
\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{rcll}\\
&&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}&\text{\footnotesize(pattern match)}\\
&&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}&\text{\footnotesize(unit, par)}\\
&&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}&\text{\footnotesize(recursion)}\\
&&\hskip-2.84526pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}\ \ {::=}\ \ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left({\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}}\right)}{=}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}&\text{\footnotesize(declaration)}\end{array}\end{array}

We assume mutually disjoint sets of

variables
(
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y},\ldots
),
and
function variables
(
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}^{\prime}},\ldots
).
In

\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\mkern 2.5mu=\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{in}}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}\right)
,
variable
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}
is bound. Similarly,

\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5mu\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}\right)
and

\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}},\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}})\mkern 2.5mu=\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{in}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp}}\right)
,
variables
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}

(
i\in I
) and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}},\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}

are bound, respectively.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}({\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp}})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}}}
binds
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}
in both

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}
.

\operatorname{fv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}\right)/\operatorname{fn}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}\right)
denote the set of free variables/names (introduced later) in

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}
.

\operatorname{ffv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}\right)
is the set of free function variables in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}
,
and
\operatorname{dfv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\right)
is the set of declared function variables in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}

(i.e.
\operatorname{dfv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left({\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}}\right)}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}\right)=\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}\}
).

()
denotes unit value and

stands for unused binding variables.

Program

includes

initiation
which generates
a series of interconnected channels
from a global combinator
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}
,
each of which corresponds to a role occurring in
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}
.
This expression corresponds to
Line 1 and Line 8 in

\Cref
fig:full:impl.

Output
expression

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}

sends label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}
with payload
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v}
via channel
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}
to role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
,
then binds the continuation to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}
,
and proceeds to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}
.

Input
expression

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}

receives on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}
from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}

then binds the received value
to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}
,
and proceeds to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}
.
The received value will have the form

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v_{1}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v_{2}}}\right)}}\right]}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v_{1}}
are the label and payload sent from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
,
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v_{2}}
is a continuation.
The received value is decomposed by

pattern matching
expression

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}
which
matches
against patterns
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}}\right)}}\right]}
(
i\in I
),
and if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}
,
it continues to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{k}}
after simultaneously substituting
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{k}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{k}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v_{1}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v_{2}}
, respectively.

Recursive function definition

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}},\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}}}
defines a recursive function
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}
with
parameters
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}},\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}
and body
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}
which is local to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}
.
A unit value
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}
represents an inactive thread.

Parallel

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}
represents two
threads running concurrently.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v}}}\right\rangle}
is the
function application
.

We use the following shorthand for expressions with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}z}
fresh:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{0}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\right\}_{i\in I}}&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}}{{=}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}z}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{0}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}z}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}}{({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}})}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{0}}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}}{{=}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}z}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{0}}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}z}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}\}}}}\end{array}

Example C.2
.

The following expressions implement the protocol
in Example
3.2
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{\mathrm{Auth}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}_{\mathrm{Auth}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\right)}}

where, with
\operatorname{fv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}\right)=\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\}
,
\operatorname{fv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}\right)=\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}}\}
,
and
\operatorname{fv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{\mathrm{Auth}}}}\right)=\{\}
,
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}

are following:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{auth}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\text{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\small{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_string}{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{"passwd"}}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{3}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\right\}}\right)}}\\

Example C.3
.

Let

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}_{Cal}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{fix}}\,{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}\,\texttt{-{}>}\,{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{choice}}\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\texttt{)}\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}x}}}}{\texttt{,}\,}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\texttt{)}\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}\ {\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{(}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}\,\rightarrow\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\texttt{)}\,\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\texttt{finish}}}}\right)}}\right\}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{0}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}\#\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}\#\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{3}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}\#\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{}}}}}}\\
&&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{4}}})}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{3}}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\right\rangle}{=}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}0}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}_{0}}}\right\rangle}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}0}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}}\right\rangle}}};\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{3}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}\#\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}}}\right\}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{\tt Cal}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{0}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}_{0}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}_{Cal}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\right)}}\end{array}

Then,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{\tt Cal}}\rightarrow\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}_{\tt Cal}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}){\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{0}}}}}\right\}}}\ \big|\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\right\rangle}{=}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}0}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}0}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}_{0}}}}}\right\}}})}}\bigr)}}

where

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}{\mathbin{.}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}{\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}})}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}{\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\right)}}\right]}\right\rangle}})}}}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}{\mathbin{.}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}{\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}})}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}{\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\right)}}}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}})}}\right]}\right\rangle}}}\\
&&\left(={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}{\mathbin{.}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}{\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}}{{\mathtt{@}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\ ]}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}\right)}}\right]}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}}}{{\mathtt{@}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\ ]}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}{\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\right)}}}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}}\right)}}\right]}}}\right]}\right\rangle}}}\right)\\
\end{array}

See that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unfold}^{\ast}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}\#\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}}\right)}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}\right)}\\
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unfold}^{\ast}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}\#\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}\right)}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}{\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\right)}}\right]}\right\rangle}}\right)}\\
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unfold}^{\ast}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}\right)}}&=&\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}\right)}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}{=}{\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\right)}}}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}}\right)}}\right]\\
&&\left(=\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}}{{\mathtt{@}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{loop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\ ]}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}\right)}}\right]}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}}}{{\mathtt{@}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\ ]}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}{=}{\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{stop}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\right)}}}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}}\right)}}\right]}}}\right]\right)\end{array}

and each time
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}
lient sends a label,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}
erver
makes an external choice between
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}}
,
and they reduce as follows:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}_{\tt Cal}}\rightarrow^{6}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{2}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}){\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}\ \big|\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\right\rangle}{=}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}0}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\right)}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}
.

C.1.2
Dynamic Semantics of
MiO

We introduce a reduction semantics of expressions,
which is a standard MPST
\pi
-calculus, with
extra handling on channel vectors.

Definition C.4
.

The reduction relation
\rightarrow
of the expressions
is defined by the rules
in Fig.
24
.
The syntax of
MiO

in
\Cref
def:program is extended to the
runtime syntax

as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}e&::=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\boldsymbol{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\boldsymbol{\#}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\boldsymbol{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}\\
&{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}\right\rangle}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){e}}\end{array}

A
reduction context

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}
is defined by the following grammar:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}::={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}}}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}({\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}}}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\ ]}}

Restriction

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){e}}

denotes session channel
s
binding all free channels in the form of

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}_{\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{{j}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{{k}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{i}}\}}

which are generated by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\llbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\rrbracket}^{s}
.
The structural congruence
\equiv
(adapted from

[
56
]
) is inductively defined by the rules in
\Cref
fig:strcong.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\qquad({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}})\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime\prime}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime\prime}})\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}\\[2.84526pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}}}\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\bigr)}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}}}\quad\text{if }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\notin\operatorname{fn}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}\right)\\[2.84526pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}\bigr)}}\quad\text{if }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\notin\operatorname{fn}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\right)\\[2.84526pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\bigr)}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}\bigr)}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\quad\text{if }\operatorname{dfv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\right)\cap\operatorname{ffv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}}\right)=\emptyset\\[2.84526pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D^{\prime}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}\bigr)}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D^{\prime}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}\bigr)}}\\[2.84526pt]
\text{if }\bigl(\operatorname{dfv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\right)\cup\operatorname{ffv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\right)\bigr)\cap\operatorname{dfv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D^{\prime}}}\right)=\bigl(\operatorname{dfv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D^{\prime}}}\right)\cup\operatorname{ffv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D^{\prime}}}\right)\bigr)\cap\operatorname{dfv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\right)=\emptyset\\[2.84526pt]
\end{array}

Figure 23:
Structural Congruence rules
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}\cdots\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{n}})}}\ \longrightarrow\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}}}}\right\}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}\cdots\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{n}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{n}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}}}}\right\}}})}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{\scriptsize[{\sc Ored-Init}]}}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\llbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\rrbracket}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}},\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{n}}}\right)}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\ \text{fresh}\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}}}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}}}\ \longrightarrow\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}}}\right\}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{2}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}}}}\right\}}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{\scriptsize[{\sc Ored-Comm}]}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}}={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}_{k}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}}\right)}}\quad{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{i}}}{{\mathtt{@}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h_{i}}}}\right]_{i\in I}}}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{2}}={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h_{k}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}]\ \ (\exists k\in I)\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5muc\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}\ \longrightarrow\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{k}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{k}}}}}\right\}}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{2}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{k}}}}}\right\}}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{\scriptsize[{\sc Ored-Match}]}}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{2}}}\right)}}\right]}\quad(\exists k\in I)\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\longrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{\scriptsize[{\sc Ored-$\equiv$}]}}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\longrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}({\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}\!\left\langle{\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}\right\rangle}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}\right)}}\longrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{letrec}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}({\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}}{{\tilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}}}}\right\}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}\right)}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{\scriptsize[{\sc Ored-Rec}]}}\hskip 230.00035pt\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}]\longrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}]\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{\scriptsize[{\sc Ored-Ctx}]}}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\longrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}\end{array}

Figure 24:
Reduction rules
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\longrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e^{\prime}}

The reduction rules of
MiO

are defined in
\Cref
fig:reduction.
Rule
[
Ored-Init
]
generates
a tuple of channel vectors

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}},\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{n}}}\right)}

with fresh name
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}

from a global combinator (
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\llbracket}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\rrbracket}
)
and then substitutes them to variables
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}
and continue to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}
.
We assume that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}
freely occurs in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}
only, but not in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{j}}
where
i\neq j
.
The names
introduced by channel vectors are bound by restriction by
s
.
In rule
[
Ored-Comm
]
,
the sender and receiver interact via two interconnected channel vectors

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}
at role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
,
respectively.
They have the form

({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}})

and

({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}})

which
communicates label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}
and payload
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}
from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
.
On sender’s side,
record projection
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}

yields
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{k}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}}\right)}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{k}}
takes a form of

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}_{\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{i^{\prime}}}\}}
.
On the receiver’s side,
evaluation of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
yields
wrapped names
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}_{i}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{i}}})}}]}_{i\in I}

where each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}_{i}}
takes a form of

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}_{\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{j^{\prime}}}\}}
.
The communication happens
if they both are generated
from the same global combinator and
interconnected via the same name
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}
and the same index
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{i^{\prime}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{j^{\prime}}}
.

After communication, the sender binds

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}
and continues to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}
.
The receiver receives the variant value

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{2}}={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h_{k}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}]={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\ ]}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{k}}})}}]}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}]={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{k}}})}}]}

which contains both received payload
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}
and continuation
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{k}}
,
and binds it to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}
and continues to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{2}}
,
and the variant value is matched in the subsequent reductions.

Rule
[
Ored-Match
]
matches
the variant values of the form

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{l}}_{\mathnormal{k}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{2}}})}}]}

yielded by
recv

against patterns
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{i}}}\right)}}\right]}_{i\in I}
,
and if
k\in I
, it
binds
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{1}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{2}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{k}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y_{k}}
respectively, and reduces to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{k}}
.

The rest of the rules are standard from
[
56
]
.
Rule
[
Ored-Rec
]
instantiates a recursive call
to its body
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}
;
Rule
[
Ored-
\equiv
]
defines a reduction up to
the structural congruence defined in

\Cref
fig:strcong. Rule
[
Ored-Ctx
]
is a contextual rule.

Example C.5

(Reduction)
.

Recall Examples
3.2
,

C.2

and
3.10
.
We have

:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}_{\mathrm{Auth}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\right)}}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{0}}}}}\right\}}}\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}_{0}}}}}\right\}}}\bigr)}}\\[2.84526pt]
={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{auth}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{\cdots}}\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{auth}}}}{({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}})}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{\cdots}}\bigr)}}\\[2.84526pt]
\left(\noindent\hbox{}{{\hbox{$\begin{array}[c]{@{}l@{}}\text{They interact on ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{3}}$, since\ }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}{=}\langle{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{auth}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{3}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}})}}}\rangle\rangle}\text{\ and\ }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}{=}{{[{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{auth}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}({\underline{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{3}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}})}}}]}}\rangle}\\[2.84526pt]

\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){\Bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{recv}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\#\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{3}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\right\}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu\textbf{send}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}\#{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mkern 2.5mu{\text{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\small{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_string}{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{"ok"}}}}}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\Bigr)}}\\[5.69054pt]
\text{(Here, the sender selects ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}$, interacting on ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s_{1}}$ and evolving to:)}\\[2.84526pt]
\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){\Bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{match}\mkern 2.5mu{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}{=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left({\text{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\small{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_string}{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{"ok"}}}}}}}}{,}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\texttt{()}}}\right)}}\right]}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{with}\mkern 2.5mu\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{ok}}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{2}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{cancel}}}}({\mbox{\lx@text@underline{\hskip 6.99997pt}}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{3}}})\triangleright{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}}}\}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}\Bigr)}}\rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}){({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet})}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bullet}.\end{array}

C.2
Static Semantics and Properties of
MiO

This section summarises the typing systems of

MiO
; then proves type soundness of

MiO
. Typing
MiO
is divided into three judgements
(channel vectors, wrappers and expressions)

Definition C.6

(Typing rules)
.

\Cref

fig:typingforchvec and
\Cref
fig:typingforexpression
give the typing rules.
We extend the syntax of typing contexts
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}

from
\Cref
def:typingcontext
as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{::=}\ldots{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}

and introduce
context for recursive functions
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}
as:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}{::=}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}{\;\text{\Large$\mid$}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{n}}}}
.
Here,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{n}}}}
states that
the parameter type of an
n
-ary function
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}
.
The typing judgement for
(1) channel vectors
has the form

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\vdash{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\mathbin{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}
;
(2) wrappers
has the form

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\vdash{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h}}\mathbin{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}H}}
where
the type for wrappers is defined as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}H}{::=}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}]
; and (3) expressions
has a form
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}
.
We assume that all types in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}
are closed.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{1}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}\cdots\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{n}})}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{\scriptsize[{\sc Ot}-Init]}}\ \operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}})=\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{{1}}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{{n}}}\}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}}\vdash_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{{1}}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{{n}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}}}\mathbin{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{1}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}}{\cdots}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\scriptstyle\times}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{n}}}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{i}}}}\quad\forall i\in\{1{,\mathinner{\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp\kern-1.99997pt\ldotp},}n\}\\[2.84526pt]

Figure 25:
The Typing Rules for Expressions
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}}}

The rules for channel vectors are standard
where the subtyping relation in rule

[
Otc-Sub
]
is defined at
\Cref
def:subtyping in
\Cref
sec:typing:global.

For wrappers,
rule
[
Otc-WrapInp
]
types wrapped names
where the payload type
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}
of input channel
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
is the same as the hole’s type,
and all wrappers have the same result type
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
.
Rule
[
Otc-Wrapper
]

checks type of a channel vector
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}]
and replaces
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}
with the hole
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\ ]}}
.

For expressions,
rule
[
Ot
-Init]
types
the initialisation with a typed global combinator.
Rule
[
Ot
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\oplus}
]
types the output expression
which sends a label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}}
and a payload
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}
with
as a nested record at
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}
.
Rule
[
Ot
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{recv}}
]
is the dual rule for the input
expression. Rule
[
Ot
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{\nu}}
]
hides
all indexed
s
by
s
.
Other rules are standard from
[
56
]
.

Example C.7

(Typing expression)
.

Recall that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{\mathrm{Auth}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{let}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x^{\prime}}}\mkern 2.5mu{=}\mkern 2.5mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{g}}_{\mathrm{Auth}}}}\mkern 2.5mu\mathbf{in}\mkern 2.5mu{\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}\right)}}
from
\Cref
ex:auth:mio.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{c}}}}}}

2.5mu{\text{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\small{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_string}{\color[rgb]{0.4,0,0}{"passwd"}}}}}}}}\mkern
